--- CAMINHO DO ARQUIVO: gerar_auditoria.py ---
Conteúdo:
----------------------------------------
import os

# Pastas que devem ser ignoradas na varredura
EXCLUDED_DIRS = {
    'node_modules', '.git', 'venv', 'env', '__pycache__', 
    'dist', 'build', '.next', '.vscode', '.idea', 'coverage'
}

# Extensões de arquivos binários ou pesados que não contêm código de texto útil
EXCLUDED_EXTENSIONS = {
    '.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.gif', '.svg', '.ico', '.webp',
    '.mp4', '.mp3', '.pdf', '.zip', '.tar', '.gz', '.lock',
    '.pyc', '.exe', '.bin', '.woff', '.woff2', '.ttf', '.eot'
}

# Arquivos específicos a serem ignorados
EXCLUDED_FILES = {
    '.env', '.env.local', 'package-lock.json', 'yarn.lock', 'pnpm-lock.yaml'
}

def generate_project_audit(output_filename="audit_projeto.txt"):
    root_dir = os.getcwd()
    print(f"Iniciando varredura no diretório: {root_dir}")
    
    file_count = 0
    
    with open(output_filename, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as out_f:
        out_f.write(f"=== AUDITORIA COMPLETA DO PROJETO ===\n")
        out_f.write(f"Diretório raiz: {root_dir}\n\n" + "="*70 + "\n\n")
        
        for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(root_dir):
            # Modifica 'dirnames' in-place para impedir que o os.walk entre em pastas excluídas
            dirnames[:] = [d for d in dirnames if d not in EXCLUDED_DIRS and not d.startswith('.')]
            
            for file in filenames:
                if file in EXCLUDED_FILES or file == output_filename:
                    continue
                
                _, ext = os.path.splitext(file)
                if ext.lower() in EXCLUDED_EXTENSIONS:
                    continue
                    
                file_path = os.path.join(dirpath, file)
                relative_path = os.path.relpath(file_path, root_dir)
                
                print(f"Processando: {relative_path}")
                
                out_f.write(f"--- CAMINHO DO ARQUIVO: {relative_path} ---\n")
                out_f.write("Conteúdo:\n")
                out_f.write("-" * 40 + "\n")
                
                try:
                    with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f:
                        content = f.read()
                        out_f.write(content)
                except Exception as e:
                    out_f.write(f"[Erro ao ler o arquivo: {e}]\n")
                    
                out_f.write("\n" + "="*70 + "\n\n")
                file_count += 1
                
    print(f"\nAuditoria concluída com sucesso! {file_count} arquivos processados.")
    print(f"Arquivo gerado na raiz: {output_filename}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    generate_project_audit()

--- CAMINHO DO ARQUIVO: README.md ---

# React + TypeScript + Vite

This template provides a minimal setup to get React working in Vite with HMR and some ESLint rules.

Currently, two official plugins are available:

- [@vitejs/plugin-react](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react) uses [Oxc](https://oxc.rs)
- [@vitejs/plugin-react-swc](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react-swc) uses [SWC](https://swc.rs/)

## React Compiler

The React Compiler is not enabled on this template because of its impact on dev & build performances. To add it, see [this documentation](https://react.dev/learn/react-compiler/installation).

## Expanding the ESLint configuration

If you are developing a production application, we recommend updating the configuration to enable type-aware lint rules:

```js
export default defineConfig([
  globalIgnores(['dist']),
  {
    files: ['**/*.{ts,tsx}'],
    extends: [
      // Other configs...

      // Remove tseslint.configs.recommended and replace with this
      tseslint.configs.recommendedTypeChecked,
      // Alternatively, use this for stricter rules
      tseslint.configs.strictTypeChecked,
      // Optionally, add this for stylistic rules
      tseslint.configs.stylisticTypeChecked,

      // Other configs...
    ],
    languageOptions: {
      parserOptions: {
        project: ['./tsconfig.node.json', './tsconfig.app.json'],
        tsconfigRootDir: import.meta.dirname,
      },
      // other options...
    },
  },
])

```

You can also install [eslint-plugin-react-x](https://npmx.dev/package/eslint-plugin-react-x) and [eslint-plugin-react-dom](https://npmx.dev/package/eslint-plugin-react-dom) for React-specific lint rules:

```js
// eslint.config.js
import reactX from 'eslint-plugin-react-x'
import reactDom from 'eslint-plugin-react-dom'

export default defineConfig([
  globalIgnores(['dist']),
  {
    files: ['**/*.{ts,tsx}'],
    extends: [
      // Other configs...
      // Enable lint rules for React
      reactX.configs['recommended-typescript'],
      // Enable lint rules for React DOM
      reactDom.configs.recommended,
    ],
    languageOptions: {
      parserOptions: {
        project: ['./tsconfig.node.json', './tsconfig.app.json'],
        tsconfigRootDir: import.meta.dirname,
      },
      // other options...
    },
  },
])

```

--- CAMINHO DO ARQUIVO: src\App.tsx ---
Conteúdo:
----------------------------------------
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import type { Team, TeamColor, Question } from './types';
import { Board } from './components/Board/Board';
import { ScoreBoard } from './components/Controls/ScoreBoard';
import { DiceRoller } from './components/Controls/DiceRoller';
import { ChallengeModal } from './components/Cards/ChallengeModal';
import questionsData from './data/questions.json';

const INITIAL_TEAMS: Team[] = [
  { id: 'red', name: 'Vermelho', colorHex: '#ED1C24', score: 0, laps: 0, position: 0 },
  { id: 'blue', name: 'Azul', colorHex: '#0055A5', score: 0, laps: 0, position: 0 },
  { id: 'green', name: 'Verde', colorHex: '#22B14C', score: 0, laps: 0, position: 0 },
  { id: 'yellow', name: 'Amarelo', colorHex: '#FFC90E', score: 0, laps: 0, position: 0 },
];

export const App: React.FC = () => {
  const [teams, setTeams] = useState<Team[]>(INITIAL_TEAMS);
  const [currentTurn, setCurrentTurn] = useState<TeamColor>('red');
  const [activeQuestion, setActiveQuestion] = useState<Question | null>(null);
  const [selectedLevel, setSelectedLevel] = useState<number>(5);
  const [lastDiceSum, setLastDiceSum] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [isFullscreen, setIsFullscreen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  
  // Novos estados para gerenciamento de perguntas e Pensa Rápido
  const [usedQuestions, setUsedQuestions] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [isPensaRapidoAtivo, setIsPensaRapidoAtivo] = useState(false);
  
  // Estado para o seletor dinâmico de categoria da casa atual
  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState<string>('');
  
  const appRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const questions: Question[] = questionsData as Question[];

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleFullscreenChange = () => {
      setIsFullscreen(Boolean(document.fullscreenElement));
    };
    document.addEventListener('fullscreenchange', handleFullscreenChange);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('fullscreenchange', handleFullscreenChange);
  }, []);

  const toggleFullscreen = () => {
    if (!appRef.current) return;
    if (!document.fullscreenElement) {
      appRef.current.requestFullscreen().catch((err) => {
        alert(`Erro ao tentar modo tela cheia: ${err.message}`);
      });
    } else {
      document.exitFullscreen();
    }
  };

  const nextTurn = () => {
    const order: TeamColor[] = ['red', 'blue', 'green', 'yellow'];
    setCurrentTurn(order[(order.indexOf(currentTurn) + 1) % order.length]);
    setLastDiceSum(null);
  };

  const handleDiceRolled = (total: number) => {
    setLastDiceSum(total);
  };

  const triggerCategoryChallenge = (category: string) => {
    const available = questions.filter(
      (q) =>
        q.level === selectedLevel &&
        q.category === category &&
        !usedQuestions.includes(q.id)
    );

    if (available.length === 0) {
      alert("As perguntas deste nível e categoria acabaram! O histórico será resetado.");
      setUsedQuestions([]);
      return;
    }

    const chosen = available[Math.floor(Math.random() * available.length)];
    setUsedQuestions([...usedQuestions, chosen.id]);
    setIsPensaRapidoAtivo(false);
    setActiveQuestion(chosen);
  };

  // Função para Desafios Especiais (Pensa Rápido e Desafio Geral)
  const triggerSpecialChallenge = (type: 'pensa_rapido' | 'desafio_geral') => {
    const levelToSearch = type === 'pensa_rapido' ? 1 : selectedLevel;
    
    const available = questions.filter(
      (q) => q.level === levelToSearch && !usedQuestions.includes(q.id)
    );
    
    if (available.length === 0) {
      alert("Perguntas esgotadas. Resetando histórico...");
      setUsedQuestions([]);
      return;
    }

    const chosen = available[Math.floor(Math.random() * available.length)];
    setUsedQuestions([...usedQuestions, chosen.id]);
    setIsPensaRapidoAtivo(type === 'pensa_rapido');
    setActiveQuestion(chosen);
  };

  // Mecânica de Sorte ou Revés interativa
  const handleSorteReves = () => {
    const isSorte = Math.random() > 0.5;
    if (isSorte) {
      alert("🍀 SORTE!\n\nAvance 2 casas ou ganhe 50 pontos extras!");
    } else {
      const defender = confirm(
        "☠️ REVÉS!\n\nPerca 50 pontos ou tente um DESAFIO PARA SE DEFENDER!\nDeseja tentar a defesa resolvendo uma questão de 1 nível ACIMA?"
      );
      if (defender) {
        const nextLevel = Math.min(selectedLevel + 1, 9);
        const available = questions.filter(
          (q) => q.level === nextLevel && !usedQuestions.includes(q.id)
        );
        if (available.length > 0) {
          const chosen = available[Math.floor(Math.random() * available.length)];
          setUsedQuestions([...usedQuestions, chosen.id]);
          setIsPensaRapidoAtivo(false);
          setActiveQuestion(chosen);
        } else {
          alert("Não há perguntas de nível superior disponíveis. Você escapou do revés!");
        }
      }
    }
  };

  return (
    <div 
      ref={appRef} 
      className={`min-h-screen bg-slate-950 text-slate-100 p-2 font-sans flex flex-col justify-between overflow-x-hidden ${isFullscreen ? 'h-screen overflow-y-auto' : ''}`}
    >
      {/* Grid de 3 Colunas Ocupando 100% da Largura da Tela */}
      <main className="w-full grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-3 items-center my-auto flex-1 px-1">
        
        {/* COLUNA ESQUERDA (lg:col-span-3) - Placar Vertical */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-3 flex flex-col gap-2 bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 p-3 rounded-2xl shadow-xl max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
          <h2 className="text-xs font-black text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest text-center border-b border-slate-800 pb-1.5">
            Placar e Turnos
          </h2>
          <ScoreBoard
            teams={teams}
            currentTurn={currentTurn}
            onUpdateScore={(id, d) => setTeams((p) => p.map((t) => (t.id === id ? { ...t, score: t.score + d } : t)))}
            onUpdateLaps={(id, d) => setTeams((p) => p.map((t) => (t.id === id ? { ...t, laps: Math.max(0, t.laps + d) } : t)))}
            onSelectTurn={(id) => setCurrentTurn(id)}
          />
        </div>

        {/* COLUNA CENTRAL (lg:col-span-6) - Tabuleiro Quadrado Gigante */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-6 flex justify-center items-center w-full">
          <Board 
            teams={teams} 
            isFullscreen={isFullscreen} 
            onToggleFullscreen={toggleFullscreen}
          >
            <div className="text-center flex flex-col items-center gap-1">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-extrabold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest">
                Vez da Equipe
              </span>
              <div
                className="px-4 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-black border shadow-md"
                style={{
                  backgroundColor: teams.find((t) => t.id === currentTurn)?.colorHex + '33',
                  borderColor: teams.find((t) => t.id === currentTurn)?.colorHex,
                  color: '#ffffff',
                }}
              >
                {teams.find((t) => t.id === currentTurn)?.name} {lastDiceSum ? `(Andou ${lastDiceSum})` : ''}
              </div>
            </div>

            <DiceRoller onDiceRolled={handleDiceRolled} />
          </Board>
        </div>

        {/* COLUNA DIREITA (lg:col-span-3) - Nível e Desafios nas Sobras */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-3 flex flex-col gap-3 max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
          
          {/* Card de Identidade e Nível / Pontuação */}
          <div className="bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 p-3.5 rounded-2xl shadow-xl flex flex-col gap-3">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 border-b border-slate-800 pb-2.5">
              <span className="text-2xl">🎲</span>
              <div>
                <h1 className="text-base font-black text-amber-400 tracking-wider">
                  MATHOPOLY
                </h1>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Ambiente Interativo</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between bg-slate-800/80 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-700">
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 uppercase">Dificuldade (Pontos):</label>
              <select
                value={selectedLevel}
                onChange={(e) => setSelectedLevel(Number(e.target.value))}
                className="bg-slate-900 text-amber-400 text-xs font-bold py-1 px-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-600 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
              >
                {[
                  { level: 1, pts: 25 },
                  { level: 2, pts: 50 },
                  { level: 3, pts: 75 },
                  { level: 4, pts: 100 },
                  { level: 5, pts: 125 },
                  { level: 6, pts: 150 },
                  { level: 7, pts: 200 },
                  { level: 8, pts: 250 },
                  { level: 9, pts: 300 },
                ].map((item) => (
                  <option key={item.level} value={item.level}>
                    Perguntas de {item.pts} pts
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Seletor de Categoria Baseado na Casa Atual */}
          <div className="bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 p-3.5 rounded-2xl shadow-xl flex flex-col gap-2">
            <label htmlFor="casa-selector" className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase text-center border-b border-slate-800 pb-2">
              Casa Atual / Categoria
            </label>

            <select
              id="casa-selector"
              value={selectedCategory}
              onChange={(e) => setSelectedCategory(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white font-medium rounded-xl shadow-inner focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 cursor-pointer text-xs"
            >
              <option value="" disabled>Selecione a categoria da casa...</option>
              <option value="Números">🔢 Números</option>
              <option value="Números Decimais">🔢 Números Decimais</option>
              <option value="Operações">➕ Operações</option>
              <option value="Frações">🍕 Frações</option>
              <option value="Porcentagem">📊 Porcentagem</option>
              <option value="Pontos, Retas e Ângulos">📐 Pontos, Retas e Ângulos</option>
              <option value="Polígonos e Triângulos">📐 Polígonos e Triângulos</option>
              <option value="Perímetros e Áreas">📏 Perímetros e Áreas</option>
              <option value="Sólidos Geométricos">📦 Sólidos Geométricos</option>
              <option value="Corpos Redondos">🥫 Corpos Redondos</option>
              <option value="Volumes e Capacidade">🧊 Volumes e Capacidade</option>
              <option value="Equações">⚖️ Equações</option>
              <option value="Incógnitas">❓ Incógnitas</option>
              <option value="Simplificação">📉 Simplificação</option>
              <option value="Valor Numérico">🔢 Valor Numérico</option>
              <option value="Linguagem Algébrica">🔤 Linguagem Algébrica</option>
              <option value="Padrões e Sequências">🔄 Padrões e Sequências</option>
              <option value="Medidas">⏱️ Medidas</option>
              <option value="Grandezas">📏 Grandezas</option>
              <option value="Raciocínio">🧠 Raciocínio</option>
              <option value="Condicionais">🔀 Condicionais</option>
              <option value="Estratégia">♟️ Estratégia</option>
              <option value="Enigmas">🔍 Enigmas</option>
            </select>

            <button
              onClick={() => triggerCategoryChallenge(selectedCategory)}
              disabled={!selectedCategory}
              className="w-full mt-1 px-6 py-2.5 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 disabled:bg-slate-800 disabled:text-slate-600 text-white font-bold rounded-xl shadow-lg transition-all text-center cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-xs"
            >
              🎯 Sortear Pergunta da Casa
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Botões de Ações Especiais (Pensa Rápido, Sorte/Revés, Desafio Geral) */}
          <div className="bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 p-3.5 rounded-2xl shadow-xl flex flex-col gap-2">
            <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase text-center border-b border-slate-800 pb-2">
              Dinâmicas e Desafios
            </h3>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 mt-1">
              <button 
                onClick={() => triggerSpecialChallenge('pensa_rapido')} 
                className="py-2.5 bg-yellow-600 hover:bg-yellow-500 text-slate-950 font-black rounded-lg text-xs uppercase tracking-wider cursor-pointer"
              >
                ⏱️ Pensa Rápido (Lvl 1)
              </button>
              <button 
                onClick={() => handleSorteReves()} 
                className="py-2.5 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white font-black rounded-lg text-xs uppercase tracking-wider cursor-pointer"
              >
                🃏 Sorte ou Revés
              </button>
              <button 
                onClick={() => triggerSpecialChallenge('desafio_geral')} 
                className="py-2.5 bg-rose-700 hover:bg-rose-600 text-white font-black rounded-lg text-xs uppercase tracking-wider shadow-rose-900/50 shadow-lg cursor-pointer"
              >
                ⚔️ Desafio Geral (Todos!)
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

        </div>

      </main>

      {/* Modal de Desafio */}
      {activeQuestion && (
        <ChallengeModal
          question={activeQuestion}
          isPensaRapido={isPensaRapidoAtivo}
          onClose={(pts) => {
            if (pts > 0) {
              setTeams((p) => p.map((t) => (t.id === currentTurn ? { ...t, score: t.score + pts } : t)));
            }
            setActiveQuestion(null);
            nextTurn();
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default App;

--- CAMINHO DO ARQUIVO: src\components\Board\Board.tsx ---
Conteúdo:
----------------------------------------
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import type { Team } from '../../types';

interface BoardProps {
  teams: Team[];
  children?: React.ReactNode;
  isFullscreen: boolean;
  onToggleFullscreen: () => void;
}

interface TokenPosition {
  x: number;
  y: number;
}

export const Board: React.FC<BoardProps> = ({ teams, children, isFullscreen, onToggleFullscreen }) => {
  const [tokenPositions, setTokenPositions] = useState<Record<string, TokenPosition>>({
    red: { x: 88, y: 88 },
    blue: { x: 92, y: 88 },
    green: { x: 88, y: 92 },
    yellow: { x: 92, y: 92 },
  });

  const [draggingTeam, setDraggingTeam] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const handleDragStart = (teamId: string) => {
    setDraggingTeam(teamId);
  };

  const handleDrop = (e: React.DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!draggingTeam) return;

    const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
    const x = ((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 100;
    const y = ((e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 100;

    setTokenPositions((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [draggingTeam]: {
        x: Math.max(3, Math.min(97, x)),
        y: Math.max(3, Math.min(97, y)),
      },
    }));

    setDraggingTeam(null);
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center w-full h-full">
      {/* Barra de Ferramentas / Tela Cheia sutil */}
      <div className="w-full flex justify-between items-center mb-1 px-1 shrink-0">
        <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400 drop-shadow">
          Arraste os peões UNO sobre as casas
        </span>
        <button
          onClick={onToggleFullscreen}
          className="px-2.5 py-0.5 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-amber-400 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg border border-slate-700 transition-colors flex items-center gap-1 shadow z-30 cursor-pointer"
        >
          {isFullscreen ? '⤫ Sair da Tela Cheia' : '⤢ Tela Cheia'}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Tabuleiro Quadrado Maximizado na Altura da Tela */}
      <div
        onDrop={handleDrop}
        onDragOver={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
        className="bg-slate-900 border-4 border-slate-700 rounded-3xl shadow-2xl relative overflow-hidden select-none bg-contain bg-no-repeat bg-center aspect-square h-[88vh] max-h-[88vh]"
        style={{
          backgroundImage: `url('${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}meu_tabuleiro.svg')`,
        }}
      >
        {/* Centro do Tabuleiro - Caixa de Dados Proporcional */}
        <div className="absolute inset-[15%] bg-slate-950/95 rounded-2xl border-2 border-slate-800 p-3 sm:p-4 flex flex-col justify-between items-center z-10 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-md overflow-hidden">
          {children}
        </div>

        {/* Peões Arrastáveis */}
        {teams.map((team) => {
          const pos = tokenPositions[team.id] || { x: 50, y: 50 };

          return (
            <div
              key={team.id}
              draggable
              onDragStart={() => handleDragStart(team.id)}
              className="w-7 h-7 sm:w-9 sm:h-9 rounded-full border-2 border-white shadow-2xl cursor-grab active:cursor-grabbing z-20 flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-black text-white transition-transform hover:scale-125 absolute"
              style={{
                backgroundColor: team.colorHex,
                left: `${pos.x}%`,
                top: `${pos.y}%`,
                transform: 'translate(-50%, -50%)',
              }}
              title={`Arrastar peão ${team.name}`}
            >
              {team.name[0]}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

--- CAMINHO DO ARQUIVO: src\components\Cards\ChallengeModal.tsx ---
Conteúdo:
----------------------------------------
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import type { Question } from '../../types';
import { ChessViewer } from './ChessViewer';
import { GeometryViewer } from './GeometryViewer';

interface ChallengeModalProps {
  question: Question;
  onClose: (awardedPoints: number) => void;
  isPensaRapido?: boolean;
}

export const ChallengeModal: React.FC<ChallengeModalProps> = ({ question, onClose, isPensaRapido }) => {
  // Se for Pensa Rápido, o tempo é 15s, senão usa o tempo da questão
  const initialTime = isPensaRapido ? 15 : question.timeLimit;
  const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = useState<number>(initialTime);
  const [showAnswer, setShowAnswer] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [timerActive, setTimerActive] = useState<boolean>(true);

  // Instâncias de áudio
  const tickSound = useRef(new Audio('https://www.myinstants.com/media/sounds/clock-ticking-sound-effect.mp3'));
  const buzzerSound = useRef(new Audio('https://www.myinstants.com/media/sounds/wrong-answer-sound-effect.mp3'));

  useEffect(() => {
    tickSound.current.volume = 0.5;
    buzzerSound.current.volume = 1.0;
    
    // Limpa os áudios ao desmontar o componente
    return () => {
      tickSound.current.pause();
      buzzerSound.current.pause();
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!timerActive || timeLeft <= 0) return;

    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setTimeLeft((prev) => {
        const newTime = prev - 1;
        
        // Toca o tic-tac nos últimos 10 segundos
        if (newTime <= 10 && newTime > 0) {
          tickSound.current.play().catch(() => {});
        }
        
        // Quando zera
        if (newTime <= 0) {
          tickSound.current.pause();
          buzzerSound.current.play().catch(() => {});
          setShowAnswer(true); // Revela a resposta automaticamente
          setTimerActive(false);
        }
        return newTime;
      });
    }, 1000);

    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [timerActive, timeLeft]);

  const handleRevealAnswer = () => {
    setShowAnswer(true);
    setTimerActive(false);
    tickSound.current.pause();
  };

  const isTimeOut = timeLeft <= 0;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50">
      <div className={`bg-slate-800 border-4 w-full max-w-lg rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-2xl flex flex-col text-white relative transition-colors ${isTimeOut ? 'border-red-600' : 'border-slate-600'}`}>
        
        {isPensaRapido && (
          <div className="absolute -top-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bg-amber-500 text-slate-900 font-black px-4 py-1 rounded-full animate-bounce">
            ⚡ PENSA RÁPIDO!
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="flex justify-between items-center border-b border-slate-700 pb-3 mb-4 mt-2">
          <div>
            <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400 px-2 py-1 rounded border border-amber-500/30">
              {question.category}
            </span>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold mt-1">{question.title}</h2>
          </div>

          <div className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg font-mono font-bold text-xl border ${
            timeLeft <= 10 && !isTimeOut ? 'bg-red-500/20 text-red-400 border-red-500/50 animate-pulse' : 
            isTimeOut ? 'bg-red-600 text-white border-red-800' : 'bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 border-slate-600'
          }`}>
            {isTimeOut ? 'ESGOTADO' : `⏱️ ${timeLeft}s`}
          </div>
        </div>

        <p className="text-xl font-medium text-slate-100 mb-6 text-center leading-relaxed">
          {question.statement}
        </p>

        {question.category === 'xadrez' && <ChessViewer fen={question.fen} />}
        {question.category === 'geometria' && <GeometryViewer svgType={question.svgType} svgParams={question.svgParams} />}

        {showAnswer ? (
          <div className="mt-2 p-4 bg-emerald-950/60 border border-emerald-500/50 rounded-xl text-emerald-200">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-emerald-400 mb-1">Gabarito Oficial:</h3>
            <p className="text-2xl font-black text-white mb-2 text-center">{question.answer}</p>
            {question.explanation && (
              <p className="text-sm text-emerald-300/80 border-t border-emerald-800/50 pt-2 mt-2">
                <strong>Explicação:</strong> {question.explanation}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <button onClick={handleRevealAnswer} className="mt-4 w-full py-3 bg-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-600 border border-slate-500 rounded-xl font-bold text-amber-400 transition-colors text-lg">
            🔍 Revelar Gabarito
          </button>
        )}

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 mt-6 pt-3 border-t border-slate-700">
          <button onClick={() => onClose(0)} className="py-3 bg-rose-600/80 hover:bg-rose-600 text-white font-bold rounded-xl text-lg">
            ❌ Errou (+0)
          </button>
          <button onClick={() => onClose(question.points)} className="py-3 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white font-bold rounded-xl text-lg">
            ✅ Acertou (+{question.points})
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React from 'react';

interface GeometryViewerProps {
  svgType: string;
  svgParams: Record<string, number | string>;
}

export const GeometryViewer: React.FC<GeometryViewerProps> = ({ svgType, svgParams }) => {
  if (svgType === 'angle') {
    const angleA = Number(svgParams.angleA) || 50;
    const labelX = String(svgParams.labelX || 'X');

    return (
      <div className="flex justify-center my-3 bg-slate-900 p-4 rounded-lg border border-slate-700">
        <svg width="240" height="120" viewBox="0 0 240 120" className="text-white">
          {/* Linha de base */}
          <line x1="20" y1="100" x2="220" y2="100" stroke="#94a3b8" strokeWidth="3" />
          {/* Vértice central */}
          <circle cx="120" cy="100" r="4" fill="#f59e0b" />
          
          {/* Linha inclinada representando o ângulo */}
          <line x1="120" y1="100" x2="180" y2="30" stroke="#f59e0b" strokeWidth="3" />

          {/* Arco do ângulo conhecido */}
          <path d="M 150 100 A 30 30 0 0 0 140 77" fill="none" stroke="#22c55e" strokeWidth="2" />
          <text x="155" y="85" fill="#22c55e" fontSize="12" fontWeight="bold">
            {angleA}°
          </text>

          {/* Arco do ângulo desconhecido (X) */}
          <path d="M 140 77 A 30 30 0 0 0 90 100" fill="none" stroke="#ef4444" strokeWidth="2" />
          <text x="95" y="75" fill="#ef4444" fontSize="14" fontWeight="bold">
            {labelX}
          </text>
        </svg>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return null;
};

--- CAMINHO DO ARQUIVO: src\components\Controls\DiceRoller.tsx ---
Conteúdo:
----------------------------------------
import React, { useState } from 'react';

interface DiceRollerProps {
  onDiceRolled: (total: number) => void;
}

const DICE_DOTS: Record<number, string> = {
  1: '⚀', 2: '⚁', 3: '⚂', 4: '⚃', 5: '⚄', 6: '⚅',
};

export const DiceRoller: React.FC<DiceRollerProps> = ({ onDiceRolled }) => {
  const [dice1, setDice1] = useState<number>(1);
  const [dice2, setDice2] = useState<number>(1);
  const [isRolling, setIsRolling] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const rollDice = () => {
    if (isRolling) return;
    setIsRolling(true);

    let rolls = 0;
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      setDice1(Math.floor(Math.random() * 6) + 1);
      setDice2(Math.floor(Math.random() * 6) + 1);
      rolls++;

      if (rolls >= 12) {
        clearInterval(interval);
        const final1 = Math.floor(Math.random() * 6) + 1;
        const final2 = Math.floor(Math.random() * 6) + 1;
        setDice1(final1);
        setDice2(final2);
        setIsRolling(false);
        onDiceRolled(final1 + final2);
      }
    }, 70);
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center my-auto">
      <div className="flex gap-4 mb-3">
        <div
          onClick={rollDice}
          className={`w-16 h-16 sm:w-20 sm:h-20 bg-slate-100 text-slate-900 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-5xl sm:text-6xl shadow-2xl border-4 border-slate-300 cursor-pointer select-none transition-transform ${
            isRolling ? 'animate-bounce' : 'hover:scale-105 active:scale-95'
          }`}
        >
          {DICE_DOTS[dice1]}
        </div>
        <div
          onClick={rollDice}
          className={`w-16 h-16 sm:w-20 sm:h-20 bg-slate-100 text-slate-900 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center text-5xl sm:text-6xl shadow-2xl border-4 border-slate-300 cursor-pointer select-none transition-transform ${
            isRolling ? 'animate-bounce' : 'hover:scale-105 active:scale-95'
          }`}
        >
          {DICE_DOTS[dice2]}
        </div>
      </div>

      <button
        onClick={rollDice}
        disabled={isRolling}
        className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-400 text-slate-950 font-black rounded-xl shadow-lg transition-all text-sm uppercase tracking-wider disabled:opacity-50"
      >
        {isRolling ? 'Rolando Dados...' : `Rolar Dados (${dice1 + dice2})`}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import type { Team, TeamColor } from '../../types';

interface ScoreBoardProps {
  teams: Team[];
  currentTurn: TeamColor;
  onUpdateScore: (teamId: TeamColor, delta: number) => void;
  onUpdateLaps: (teamId: TeamColor, delta: number) => void;
  onSelectTurn: (teamId: TeamColor) => void;
}

export const ScoreBoard: React.FC<ScoreBoardProps> = ({
  teams,
  currentTurn,
  onUpdateScore,
  onUpdateLaps,
  onSelectTurn,
}) => {
  return (
    <div className="w-full flex flex-col gap-2">
      {teams.map((team) => {
        const isCurrentTurn = team.id === currentTurn;

        return (
          <div
            key={team.id}
            onClick={() => onSelectTurn(team.id)}
            className={`p-2 rounded-xl border-2 cursor-pointer transition-all flex flex-col justify-between ${
              isCurrentTurn
                ? 'border-white scale-[1.01] shadow-md shadow-amber-500/20'
                : 'border-slate-700 opacity-80 hover:opacity-100'
            }`}
            style={{ backgroundColor: team.colorHex + '22', borderColor: team.colorHex }}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-0.5">
              <span className="font-bold text-white text-xs">{team.name}</span>
              {isCurrentTurn && (
                <span className="bg-white text-slate-900 text-[8px] font-extrabold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full uppercase">
                  Vez
                </span>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="text-lg font-black text-white mb-1.5">
              {team.score} <span className="text-[9px] font-normal text-slate-300">pts</span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 border-t border-slate-700/50 pt-1.5 text-[10px] text-slate-300">
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                <span>Voltas: {team.laps}</span>
                <div className="flex gap-1" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => onUpdateLaps(team.id, -1)}
                    className="px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 rounded text-white font-bold cursor-pointer"
                    title="Diminuir volta"
                  >
                    -V
                  </button>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => onUpdateLaps(team.id, 1)}
                    className="px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-600 rounded text-white font-bold cursor-pointer"
                    title="Adicionar volta"
                  >
                    +V
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex justify-between items-center" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
                <span className="text-slate-400">Pontos:</span>
                <div className="flex gap-1">
                  <button
                    onClick={() => onUpdateScore(team.id, -50)}
                    className="px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-rose-900/60 hover:bg-rose-800 rounded text-white font-bold cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    -50
                  </button>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => onUpdateScore(team.id, 50)}
                    className="px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-600 rounded text-white font-bold cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    +50
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
};

--- CAMINHO DO ARQUIVO: src\types\index.ts ---
Conteúdo:
----------------------------------------
export type TeamColor = 'red' | 'blue' | 'green' | 'yellow';

export interface Team {
  id: TeamColor;
  name: string;
  colorHex: string;
  score: number;
  laps: number;
  position: number; // Índice da casa no tabuleiro (0 a N)
}

export type ChallengeCategory = 
  | 'geometria' 
  | 'algebra' 
  | 'operacoes' 
  | 'xadrez' 
  | 'damas' 
  | 'graficos';

export interface BaseQuestion {
  id: string;
  level: number;
  tags?: string[];
  category: string;
  title: string;
  statement: string;
  answer: string;
  explanation?: string;
  timeLimit: number;
  points: number;
}

export interface GeometryQuestion extends BaseQuestion {
  category: 'geometria';
  svgType: 'angle' | 'triangle' | 'rectangle' | 'circle';
  svgParams: Record<string, number | string>;
}

export interface ChessQuestion extends BaseQuestion {
  category: 'xadrez';
  fen: string; // Forsyth-Edwards Notation da posição
  solutionMove: string; // Exemplo: "Nxd5" ou "Qxf7#"
}

export interface StandardQuestion extends BaseQuestion {
  category: 'algebra' | 'operacoes' | 'damas' | 'graficos';
  formula?: string; // Expressão em LaTeX se necessário
}

export type Question = GeometryQuestion | ChessQuestion | StandardQuestion;

export interface BoardCell {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  category: ChallengeCategory | 'start' | 'bonus' | 'luck';
  description?: string;
}
======================================================================
